| Notes | This is the 'descriptor' half of [WP-03.03](../../work-packages/03-contract-foundation-and-licence-split.md#rule-wp-03.03). Registry 04 explicitly allocates the missing initial wire fields; ContractVersion identity is separate from ContractCompatibility support minima. Their version/minimum strings use registry 04 section 3 explicit SemVer profile: 128 ASCII maximum, build metadata retained in exact identity but ignored by precedence; other Key fields remain unchanged. ContextProvider/ContextDescriptor belong to PublicApi; no Foundation-to-PublicApi import cycle. ApplicationPresence and DeviceCapabilityView remain CON.11, with no InstancePresence duplicate. EncodedBodyReader validates expected type/descriptor/snapshot, canonical expiry, <=64MiB and exact byteLength plus ResourceVersionRef contentHash before invoking a caller-supplied statically generated parser; it neither fetches nor authorizes content. No new dependencies or projects. Small and foundational: many later domain tasks (CON.06 in-process ports, CON.10 task/chat tool binding) reference CapabilityDescriptor for tool metadata, so this should land early even though nothing strictly blocks it from running in parallel with CON.03-CON.05. Security support is limited to actually observed generic-api-key false positives for CON.02 public SHA256 inventory entries; bind each exact containing path, JSON key and recomputed value to its observed commit, including necessary immutable historical snapshots. Rebase or normalization may update an exception only after an actual scan finding and independent public-input hash verification; no blanket key, path, digest pattern or scanner bypass is authorized. The actual multi-file PublicApi descriptor closure requires the ContractSet evidence clarification in wp02-04-version-identity-profile.md: preserve one namespace/major identity with every source digest, not fabricated namespaces or version changes. This explicitly authorizes the bounded metadata generator algorithm and its tests; it is not an ADP.07 input-binding exception or a change to runtime schema semantics. Planning repair 2026-10-08 ([DLV-34](../README.md#rule-dlv-34); [P2-021](../../../decisions/phase-2-specification-decisions.md#rule-p2-021)): successor CON.40 (C#-only SDK standardization): the TypeScript encoded-body generation and the Kotlin generated outputs named in this record retire to C# under CON.40 (C#-only SDK standardization); the C# EncodedBodyReader stays. Outcome, validation and evidence above are recorded history and are unchanged. Planning repair 2026-10-08 ([DLV-34](../README.md#rule-dlv-34); [P2-023](../../../decisions/phase-2-specification-decisions.md#rule-p2-023)): macOS is outside the delivery scope. The macOS wording in this complete record is a negative scope statement and no macOS artifact was delivered, so nothing is migrated. Outcome, validation and evidence are unchanged. |

<a id="task-con-03"></a>

### CON.03 — Resource/Sync owner-body admission: closed Sync mutation allowlist + cross-owner/wrong-revision/opaque-object/forbidden-path negatives

**Outcome.** A generated/schema-derived validator enforces that Sync (registry04 §10) accepts client-origin writes only for authorized ScopeProjectMetadata and ScopeMetadata (the closed client-writable subset of AggregateBody), refusing TaskSnapshot/AutomationView/ConversationBody/AgentProfile/SkillRecord/ChatProjectRecord/MemoryRecord/PreferenceRecord as client writes (Cloud-authored-only); externalBody indirection resolves to the same allowlist before validation. Independent negative vectors cover cross-owner reference, wrong-revision precondition, opaque/unknown AggregateBody variant, and forbidden-path (non-allowlisted body kind) attempts, plus compatible unknown-response preservation. Author ScopeProjectMetadata and its additive AggregateBody variant, with cross-workspace project/session and tombstone vectors; project identity/name is never inferred from an arbitrary session. Append the ScopeProjectMetadata sample and exactly two valid positives to the generic public Foundation fixture: the standalone ScopeProjectMetadata-complete case and one AggregateBody case whose body.scopeProjectMetadata references that sample, so the existing exhaustive oneof-branch gate covers the additive variant. Add the exact C# Foundation dispatch mapping; append only the ScopeProjectMetadata seed and its exact record/field/generated-output ownership row to eng/foundation-inventory.json, and add only ScopeProjectMetadata to eng/check_foundation.py EXTRA_SEEDS so existing coverage accepts it. Do not add it to foundationTypes or alter checker logic/algorithm or generator behavior.

<a id="task-con-07"></a>

### CON.07 — Identity/session/device operation registry + native-auth and browser HTTP exceptions

**Outcome.** IdentityService/WorkspaceService/DeviceService are generated with every current operation in registry04 section 5, exact request/response field tags and eight authorization fields per operation. Native authorize/token and all browser session and OIDC callback exceptions in contracts07 section 1 receive generated schemas and encoding validation: native token requests remain application/x-www-form-urlencoded with the specified 16 KiB bound, and JSON bodies use the accepted strict serializer posture. Independent fixtures cover account creation, email/passkey/OIDC login, enrollment, recovery, step-up, refresh, logout and PAT journeys; existing transfer HTTP schemas remain intact.

Do not allocate the next number until the reservation merges/releases/supersedes; any rebase/change requires reclaim, pointer/N revalidation, regeneration, reservation update and invalidates prior review. CON.07 repair of rows Registry04 and catalogue 01 leave without an explicit risk or step-up, fixed here so generation never infers them: the nine anonymous or refresh-token operations (identity.beginAuthentication, completeAuthentication, requestEmailCode, redeemEmailCode, refreshSession, beginRecovery, completeRecovery, completeEnrollment, listAuthProviders) use profile one-use-auth with actor kind preauth, risk R1, no step-up, approval none and egress none; identity.beginStepUp and completeStepUp are human-owner session NI R1 without step-up; identity.beginEmailChange, completeEmailChange and changePassword are session NI R3 with step-up for beginEmailChange and changePassword only; identity.requestAccountDeletion and workspace.requestDataDeletion are the only R4 rows and require step-up; device.* rows keep scope application-target and every other row scope account; every operation keeps capability null, localPresence false and egress none and only workspace.list and workspace.get are PAT eligible; Q operations carry the optional encodedBody outcome tag 4. Field profiles: product fields use the closed arcscope/companion ProductId set while platform stays a Key; WebAuthn byte fields are individually bounded (credentialId 1023, clientDataJson, attestationObject and authenticatorData 16 KiB, signature 256, userHandle 64) so the 64 KiB aggregate cannot be exceeded; password setup values accept 15 to 128 Unicode scalars; AuthChallenge requires recoveryMethod and omits method exactly for purpose recover. HTTP exceptions are two closed schema bundles: public/http/v1/browser.schema.json (strict JSON precise-value projections of the numbered Identity records for the eight /session/v1 POST routes, the bootstrap response and the OIDC provider callback query) and public/http/v1/native-auth.schema.json (PKCE authorize query, application/x-www-form-urlencoded token request bounded to 16 KiB, the redirect callback queries and the NativeSession token response); each bundle also generates a closed route table with method and path, credential, Origin/CSRF, Set-Cookie and no-store requirements, and native client/redirect pairs are exactly the contracts07 registry. Existing transfer HTTP schemas are untouched. CON.07 amendment (review of d0ebf94): Registry04 section 1 puts required scalars and enums outside a oneof under proto3 optional, so the twelve required enum fields (AuthChallenge/NativeSession/SessionView/BeginAuthentication/RequestEmailCode purpose, AuthProviderView/BeginAuthentication/BeginStepUp method, BeginRecovery method, WorkspaceView protection, DeviceView/SetTrust trust) carry explicit presence; validation is unchanged (absent and unspecified are both refused). The registry gives no bound for these fields, so the closure fixes them here and they freeze with the first release: SessionView.workspaceIds 100, DeviceView.installed 64, DeviceCapabilityView.reasons 64, scopes, capabilities and deletion/storage/job counts 200 items, WebAuthn transports 8 and allowed/excluded credentials 64, redirectUrl 4096 UTF-8 bytes, AuthProof.emailCode, recoveryCode and password and EnrollmentProof codes 128 characters versus RedeemEmailCode.code and CompleteEmailChange.code 8192 bytes (V1 has one workspace per user, so none is restrictive). The GET browser.oidcCallback route is a cross-site provider redirect that carries no Origin header: its route origin is none, and exact-configured applies only to the cookie-bearing POST routes and the bootstrap GET. Evidence class: the field oracle, boundary vectors and frozen journey expectations are offline vectors; the journeys are declarative and not executed, and the Kotlin lite runtime has no descriptors or generated validators, so the Kotlin consumer pins explicit presence on generated builders and is compiled only by the consumer harness no CI job runs. Planning repair 2026-10-08 ([DLV-34](../README.md#rule-dlv-34); [P2-021](../../../decisions/phase-2-specification-decisions.md#rule-p2-021)): successor CON.40 (C#-only SDK standardization): the TypeScript and Kotlin generated outputs and the Dokka line named in this record retire under CON.40; the C# identity registry stays. Outcome, validation and evidence above are recorded history and are unchanged. |

<a id="task-con-10"></a>

### CON.10 — Task/approval/bridge/chat/agent/automation/search operation registry + ai-internal package

**Outcome.** All listed public services generated with exact fields and eight authorization fields. CON.10 authors ExecutionOwner (registry04), ApplicationTarget and ChatTurnProgress (annex10), ChatTurnState/ChatTurnView and the entire ToolRequest including targetApplication tag 16 in chat.proto; existing content.proto records remain canonical imports. CON.10 alone authors SessionBinding (contracts05 section 8) in internal/ai-http/v1/schema.json and exactly the 14 C# run HTTP ports authorize/claim/renew/reconcile/context/model-intent/model-outcome/settle/prepare-tools/cloud-tool/wait/finalize/stream-state/late-outcome from contracts05 section 3. OR request alternatives for those routes are represented by named Ai*Request titled oneOf roots and generated route-union codecs, never as multiple raw branch codecs left for callers to guess; branches remain exactly-one closed alternatives. These closed JSON records feed the existing ai-internal and CloudInternal packages. stream-state carries run identity, streamId, attemptId, state and nextOffset, not StreamPosition or StreamFrame. Inference ports belong solely to CON.15; public and private stream framing belongs to CON.11. The registered public SourceService policy/consent operations are authored here, importing CON.06 shared records without adding private in-process methods. Public approval.decide is the only public binding admitted by the distinct public-human-approval-decision profile: kind=proto, binding=arcforges.publicapi.v1.ApprovalService/Decide, source=public/proto/arcforges/publicapi/v1/chat.proto, scope=assistant, surface=public, sourceRule=docs/architecture/contracts/01-public-api-operations.md#rule-tk-02, idempotency=IW. Its exact eight-field authorization is capability=null, risk=verifiedApprovalProposal.effectiveRisk, approval=foregroundProposal, stepUp=verifiedApprovalProposal.stepUp, localPresence=verifiedApprovalProposal.localPresence, egress=none, patEligible=false, actorKinds=[human]. The underlying proposal is bound and validated per [TK-02](../../../architecture/contracts/01-public-api-operations.md#rule-tk-02); keep the separate local-only in-process IChatOperations.SubmitApproval human-approval-decision binding unchanged. This is static metadata admission only; no runtime authorization behavior is added.

<a id="task-con-11"></a>

### CON.11 — Application/history/execution/events operations (annex10's 13 additions) + EventService.Poll

**Outcome.** Generate exactly 14 public methods: ApplicationService.List/Heartbeat/Disconnect and HistoryService.BeginImport/FinalizeImport/GetImport/CancelImport in arcforges.publicapi.v1/application.proto; ExecutionService.StartTransientTurn/ReadOutput/WatchOutput/AcknowledgeOutput/PurgeTransient and EventService.Poll/Watch in arcforges.events.v1/events.proto. EventService.Poll remains the Registry04 operation with its own events.poll / resource-owner operation-scope and authorization-export rows (never alias events.watch), using the explicit Q/R1/AO pure-query profile and eight-field effective profile fixed in annex10 §8. Reuse existing ApplicationScope, RequestMeta.applicationScope tag 8, ConversationView.applicationScope tag 10 and historyMode tag 11, and HistoryMode in their current owning packages. Import CON.10 ExecutionOwner/ApplicationTarget/ChatTurnProgress and the complete ToolRequest without duplicate definitions or field appends. Generate the 17 EventService.Poll hint payloads from one event registry and the public StreamPosition/StreamFrame/OutputChunk/StreamReset/ExecutionProgress/ExecutionOutput closure. Generate the private RunStream projection in internal/proto/arcforges/cf/v1/stream.proto from annex10 section 7 as RunStreamService.Run(RunStreamRequest) returns (stream arcforges.events.v1.StreamFrame), where RunStreamRequest is execution: arcforges.publicapi.v1.ExecutionOwner = 1, attemptId: arcforges.foundation.v1.Id = 2, and generation: uint64 = 3. These fields bind an already authenticated private execution attempt; they are not a caller grant or a new public route. Reuse public StreamFrame variants; keep internal-to-public imports one-way and cap each encoded frame/output chunk at 32KiB. Exclude reserved future Hub/DeviceSso methods from active registration. TypeScript, C# and Kotlin executable consumers directly load the independently authored public fixture, consume every vector exactly once and verify generated descriptors/RPCs/fields; C# additionally directly loads the private RunStream fixture and verifies the private projection, one-way imports and frame limits. Missing, duplicate, unconsumed or unknown cases fail.

**Outcome.** internal/proto/arcforges/operator/v1/operator.proto generated with all 31 OperatorService methods: the 29 operator.* rows in Registry04 §9.1 plus catalog.review (ReviewCatalogSubmission) and catalog.revoke (RevokeCatalogVersion) from §5. OperatorCallContext(tag100)/OperatorProposalRef(tag101) are appended per method, with the typed propose/approve/execute protocol (§9.2) and its 9 mutation-variant table rows (grant/revokeGrant/issueCredit/adjustCredit/refund/catalogReview/catalogRevoke/appeal/kill); independent negative vectors cover distinct-approver violation, stale hash/revision/configuration, role revocation, expiry, concurrent consumption and lost receipt; no direct SQL or public-SDK import of operator schema.

<a id="task-con-15"></a>

### CON.15 — Cloudflare-internal HTTP and D1 ExecutePlan bindings

**Outcome.** Generate the owned contracts05 object routes, Worker adapter and six inference HTTP port families as closed JSON schemas in the distinct internal/cf-http/v1/schema.json source. Include the §9 job-grant/job-authorize ports and typed bounded Worker GET/PUT object facade: stable grant/owner/fence identity, expiry <=60 seconds and no later than the owner lease, service HMAC plus matching grant, per-range/part reauthorization, and streamed parts <=8 MiB; no ambient customer bearer, arbitrary object key or raw R2 credential. CON.15 is the sole inference-family author; consume only the evidence-bound canonical CON.10 definitions without copying or changing their meaning. Register only the CF and separate D1 storage schemas with the existing generator and the two existing CloudInternal/ai-internal package identities; external-reference resolution is pinned to the exact direct CON.10 path/definition allowlist in notes and fails closed otherwise. Independent offline vectors cover duplicate dispatch, lease takeover, R2 part mismatch and stale-epoch rejection. The private D1 ExecutePlan request/reply remains a separate internal storage HTTP schema from the authoritative named-plan execution profile, with plan/parameter/result typing and fail-closed guard/error vectors; it accepts no public SQL or duplicate C# business decisions. No live Worker, provider adapter implementation, authorization handler or storage implementation is included.

The focused test must inspect the actual config and observed set, prove exact match counts and path/digest pairing, and reject altered key/digest/path, appended credentials, unrelated 64-hex strings and every other rule. No blanket suppression, generic hash rule, whole-file/commit exception, scanner/workflow/rule/test algorithm change, or new dependency is authorized. CON.22's content.proto append merges before CON.24; CON.24 must rebase on that merge and append only its two summary records under serialized schema-source ownership. CON.22 also precedes CON.14, which imports the canonical public records without internal duplicates. No extra route, field, record, package, dependency, generated target, algorithm, generalized matcher/parser/generator behavior or runtime change is authorized. Planning repair 2026-10-08 ([DLV-34](../README.md#rule-dlv-34); [P2-021](../../../decisions/phase-2-specification-decisions.md#rule-p2-021)): successor CON.40 (C#-only SDK standardization): the TypeScript generated outputs and the Kotlin generated outputs and cases named in this record retire under CON.40. Outcome, validation and evidence above are recorded history and are unchanged. Planning repair 2026-10-09 ([P2-026](../../../decisions/phase-2-specification-decisions.md#rule-p2-026); scope correction): reduced: the TypeScript and Kotlin generated outputs and vectors are out of scope, not completed (CON.40, C# only); the support, notification, data, preference, policy-bundle and export-job operations remain. |

<a id="task-con-23"></a>

### CON.23 — Retire the contract and naming elements outside the product family

**Outcome.** eng/policy/product-names.json and the naming checks name only the family's products; the two retired in-process port packages and their proto files leave the package set and are never republished; retired foundation and content records, AggregateBody variants and enum values are deleted with their field, value and message numbers reserved exactly as the wire registry lists; accepted fixtures and generated C#/TypeScript/Kotlin shapes are regenerated without them; already published package versions remain immutable history. Publish the canonical product-names policy and its scanner as an immutable build-time asset in the existing Contracts candidate lifecycle, bound to the same source/version and original publication receipt; consumers pin this artifact, never a sibling checkout or a new authored naming authority. Stage the same authored scanner/data into the existing @arcforges/proto and ArcForges.Contracts.Validation packages as build-only tools; no new package identity or authored policy copy is introduced. Packaging is a build-time delivery mechanism only, not a business/runtime schema prerequisite or new naming authority.

### CON.25 — Public ConnectorService, the local call context and the af-segment records

**Outcome.** ConnectorService is generated in the public arcforges.publicapi.v1 package (public/proto/arcforges/publicapi/v1/connector.proto) with the six connector.* operations and their public ConnectorDefinition, ConnectorConnection, ConnectorChallenge and ConnectorProof records, exact fields, authorization fields and independent positive and negative vectors in C#, TypeScript and Kotlin, so that the operation-scope gate reports the six former pending rows as registered and zero pending rows; the public records are authored in the public package because a public proto never imports internal or local schema (registry 04 section 10), and the local ConnectorBrokerService records of CON.05 stay unchanged with a shape-parity check against the public records. LocalCallContext is authored in the local platform package with its exact fields, the 4 KiB and 32-root bounds and vectors. SimulationDataSegment, SimulationSample, SimulationEvent and SimulationGap are authored as proto messages in the CON.21 simulation package with the registry field numbers and oneof presence, together with the canonical af-segment.v1 JSON profile (the segment body is canonical UTF-8 JSON, never protobuf encoder output) and golden vectors that fix the exact bytes, so that the native reader and the simulator consume one published Contracts encoding.

Decisions recorded here (no Design behavior change; additive Contracts closure): (1) The public ConnectorService records are authored as public records in connector.proto because the existing ConnectorDefinition, ConnectorConnection, ConnectorChallenge and ConnectorProof messages live only in the internal arcforges.local.platform.v1 package and public proto never imports internal packages (registry 04 section 10); the internal ConnectorBrokerService of CON.05 stays a private helper boundary, unchanged and already published, so the two sets are kept in shape parity by a test and unifying them is not attempted (a published local package is not rewritten). Not in scope and not invented: no Cloud runtime owner exists for ConnectorService and none is created here. The provider callback HTTP exception schema was not determined to belong to this closure and is not authored here. <a id="task-con-40"></a>

### CON.40 — C#-only SDK standardization: stop TypeScript and Kotlin/Maven publication after consumer migration; keep @arcforges/ai-internal

**Outcome.** Contracts publishes C# as the only first-party SDK language for business clients. The Contracts TypeScript and Kotlin generators, generated outputs and tests are removed except for the single internal thin-adapter package @arcforges/ai-internal, which keeps its dedicated pipeline. New publication of @arcforges/proto, @arcforges/api-client, @arcforges/contract-fixtures, @arcforges/operator-client, Maven contracts-proto, contracts-connect-client and contract-fixtures stops only after each identity has no first-party consumer left (the stop-publication step for each identity is recorded separately). Already published versions stay immutable and resolvable, and nothing is unpublished, deleted or deprecated without explicit user approval. The Contracts TypeScript and Kotlin tests (tests/public/*.mjs, tests/public/*.ts, tests/public/HelloClient, tests/public/KotlinConnectClient, and the Kotlin consumer cases) are migrated one-for-one to C# structure, architecture and serialization tests with the same negative fixtures, limits and budgets. The C# CON.17 canonical semantic hash, golden vectors and compatibility windows are unchanged. The release docs (docs/releasing.md, docs/maven-central.md, docs/kotlin-artifacts-plan.md, docs/kotlin-grpc-web-plan.md, docs/consuming.md), policies and the dependency-admission and contract-access records are updated to the C#-only channel list. The five ContentSandbox PDF RPCs (OpenPdf, GetPdfPage, ExtractPdfText, RenderPdfTile and ClosePdf) and their PDF-named messages stay in the published arcforges.local.sandbox.v1 schema unchanged; the Contracts sandbox README and the contract-access record mark the five RPCs retired, and no new consumer may call them ([P2-022](../../../decisions/phase-2-specification-decisions.md#rule-p2-022); CON.04 retirement marking, owned by this task).

<a id="task-con-41"></a>

### CON.41 — Publish the public operation metadata in the PublicApi package

**Outcome.** Every published ArcForges.Contracts.PublicApi candidate carries, at the fixed package path operations/operation-metadata.v1.json, the public operation-metadata.v1 rows of eng/operations: exactly the rows whose surface is public and whose binding names a method of the package's descriptor closure, each value-identical to its merged source row, in a stable order. Private-helper, in-process, cf-internal and operator rows are never staged. The package source.json binds the SHA256 of every contributing eng/operations file, and the candidate verification refuses a missing, extra or changed row, so consumers pin and read the rows exactly as they read the packaged schemas, never from Contracts source. No row, field, operation, authorization value, package identity, channel or candidate format changes. The rows reach consumers through the Contracts main push (RES-contracts-publication); Cloud moves its ArcForges.Contracts.PublicApi, CloudInternal and Validation pins and its @arcforges/ai-internal pin to the first Contracts main candidate that contains this task through its own consumer tasks, never to 1.0.0-ci.350.1.

**Outcome.** The Contracts repository no longer carries dead configuration of the retired pipelines. eng/documentation_tools.py is deleted, and the tests that used it become retirement or data assertions, keeping the six frozen Dokka successor-delta tests (with hashlib in place of the tool's sha helper). Every fail-closed Gradle and Maven guard (the admission, licence and serialization checks, the mavenVersion refusal, the retirement assertions), the dated documents (docs/maven-central.md and the others), the retirement receipts, the Gradle wrapper and Dokka records bound by active receipts, provenance, releases and ledger evidence are kept. The merge publishes the normal Contracts cohort (RES-contracts-publication).

<a id="task-con-43"></a>

### CON.43 — Generated local-operation decoders

**Outcome.** The pinned Contracts generator owns the [DP-03](../../../architecture/contracts/02-local-rpc-operations.md#rule-dp-03) production decoder generation. It reads each authored local-RPC request and service descriptor and its declared operation profile (the operation-metadata.v1 row of eng/operations that binds the operation) and, for each operation, emits into the C# LocalRpc package that holds the descriptor (ArcForges.Contracts.LocalRpc.Platform, Chat, Scope or Sandbox): the descriptor's schema; the closed allowlist entry through which a CapabilityKey resolves ([DP-04](../../../architecture/contracts/02-local-rpc-operations.md#rule-dp-04)); the non-reflective decode function from the generated CapabilityArguments to the typed request, which refuses an unknown field, a missing required field and an out-of-bounds value as a typed protocol error before any owner code runs ([DP-05](../../../architecture/contracts/02-local-rpc-operations.md#rule-dp-05)); and the result encoder from the typed result to the generated CapabilityResult. The emitted code uses only Contracts types and no reflection, MakeGenericType or assembly scanning ([DP-06](../../../architecture/contracts/02-local-rpc-operations.md#rule-dp-06)), so a consumer binds it to the ArcForges.Capabilities decoder and encoder delegates by method group. An operation without a declared profile fails generation, and generate --check refuses drift. The decoders reach consumers through the Contracts main push (RES-contracts-publication); the first Contracts main candidate that contains this task and CON.41 is the candidate PLT.58 and APP.03 adopt (S59(7)). No operation, field, authorization value, package identity or channel changes.
